    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();

    try {

      if (encoding == null) {

        isr = new InputStreamReader(stream);

      } else {

        isr = new InputStreamReader(stream, encoding);
99 
      }
100 
      try (BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(isr)) {
101 
        String line;
102 
        while ((line = br.readLine()) != null) {
103 
          sb.append(line);
104 
          sb.append('\n');
105 
        }
106 
      }
107 
    } finally {
108 
      if (isr != null) {
109 
        isr.close();
110 
      }
111 
    }
112

  }

  /**

   * Escapes these characters: less than, greater than, quote, ampersand.
310 
   */
311 
  public static String escapeHTML(String s) {
312 
    // this version is much faster than using s.replaceAll()
313 
    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();

    int n = s.length();
315 
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
316 
      char c = s.charAt(i);
317 
      switch (c) {
318 
        case '<':
319 
          sb.append("&lt;");
320 
          break;
321 
        case '>':
322 
          sb.append("&gt;");
323 
          break;
324 
        case '&':
325 
          sb.append("&amp;");
326 
          break;
327 
        case '"':
328 
          sb.append("&quot;");
329 
          break;
330 
        default:
331 
          sb.append(c);
332 
          break;
333 
      }
334 
    }
335 
    return sb.toString();

  }

    }
